HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_005657ce2783c2b4fb60","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:6:1\",\"root_001564\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateşin uyandırdığı kaçma ve geri çekilme isteği, sonraki kapatma ve sütunlarla engellenir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001672/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_04998dab9c110f1bc71f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:6:3\",\"root_001672\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Biriktirilmiş servet ve saldırgan davranışların, güvence olmak yerine ateşi besleyen malzemeye dönüşebileceği ileri sürülür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B001","root_001672/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_07d65b10b56cb36838b7","text":"2. **A fire sustained by what was accumulated.** Fixed `ٱلْمُوقَدَةُ` activates `و ق د B002`, the material consumed as fuel. The focus window supplies gathered wealth (`ج م ع B001`, `م و ل B001`), repeated social pressure (`ه م ز B003`, `ل م ز B002`), and the kindled result. The packet never identifies a fuel, so the arrow is explicitly abductive: accumulated property and accumulated aggression are candidate inputs that sustain the consuming system they were expected to resist. The fire can therefore read as self-fed by the hoarder's own economy and practice, not as an unrelated external image.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B001","root_001564/B002","root_001672/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0ad3694ed720a22cc19f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:6:1\",\"root_001564\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"104:6:3\",\"root_001672\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateş, yürekteki gizli yönelimi yalnızca yakmaz; aydınlatıp görünür kılar ve sahibinin üzerine işaret gibi işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001672/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_394370f993a496ecda26","text":"2. **Fuel reversal of stored wealth.** Evidence: `و ق د B002` (fuel/firewood) and `م و ل B001`, activated by `ج م ع B001` and `ع د د B002` in 104:2. Structural cue: stores prepared for security precede a fire described by its kindling. Assigned role: وقود supplies the consumable material side of the fire. Abductive move: what was stored as provision can be reread as potential fuel before divine judgment. Reading change: wealth loses its role as reserve and becomes combustible supply.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_454aa49edf1de5639869","text":"1. **Divine fire as branding disclosure.** Evidence: `ن و ر B002` (burning fire and branding by it), `و ق د B001` (fire kindled), and `ء ل ه B001/B002`; activated by the name-frame of 104:0 and the social-name injury of 104:1. Structural cue: Allah reappears between نار and موقدة. Assigned role: the fire marks under divine authority. Abductive move: since the offender marked others by contemptuous speech, divine fire marks him with the truth of his act. Reading change: the fire is not merely heat; it is a divine branding that exposes and fixes moral reality.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B007","root_001672/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5f7b1de66f97d78196d5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:6:1\",\"root_001564\",[\"B007\"]],[\"104:6:3\",\"root_001672\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İnsanlar arasında karalama ve ayıplamayla alevlenen düşmanlık, burada bütünüyle tutuşmuş ve kendi iç kaynağına dönmüş bir sürece dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001672/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6920dafd2e1b708bda7e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:6:3\",\"root_001672\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sonraki kapanma ve destek yapısı, bu ateşi geçici bir alev değil, sürekli tutulan denetimli bir düzenek hâline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_001564/B002","root_001672/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6abab4b3b4d50fd93578","text":"1. **A fire that reveals and marks the inward target.** Fixed `نَارُ` directly realizes `ن و ر B002` (burning fire and branding), while activating `ن و ر B001` (illumination); fixed `ٱلْمُوقَدَةُ` realizes `و ق د B001` (kindling). `104:7` adds disclosure through `ط ل ع B003` and a heart reached through `ف ء د B003`. The genitive `ٱللَّهِ`, the fixed divine name of `ء ل ه B002`, supplies attribution, not by itself a causal theory. I infer that illumination and branding give the attributed fire a diagnostic role: it reaches, exposes, and marks the inward center rather than operating as undirected heat.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_78d55b2c6d28fdfdec0b","text":"4. **The impulse to recoil blocked by closure.** Fixed `نَارُ` also activates `ن و ر B006`, aversion, flight, and recoiling. `104:8` supplies a sealed door-state through `و ص د B001`, and `104:9` supplies extended columns through `ع م د B003` and `م د د B001`. The packet gives the aversive fire and the blocked enclosure; I infer that the natural movement away from fire is deliberately frustrated. The reading shifts from fire as a painful object to fire as the center of a trap whose architecture prevents the response it provokes.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_916e66f47c04de6edeaa","text":"`104:7` changed illumination and branding from surface effects into inward disclosure by naming the hearts as endpoint. `104:8`-`104:9` then made `ن و ر B006` unexpectedly structural: recoil is evoked by fire but defeated by sealing and bracing. The later ayat therefore corrected a free-burning image into a controlled system whose energy, target, and blocked escape are separately specified.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001672/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_996d779c12575d1006d8","text":"4. **Flaring intensity against social hostility.** Evidence: `و ق د B005` (ready spark, intensity, anger/war), activated by `ن و ر B007` (enmity flaring between people) and `ه م ز/ل م ز` in 104:1. Structural cue: social hostility at the start is answered by a fire already kindled. Assigned role: توقد supplies intensified response. Abductive move: human social fire—enmity, contempt, provocation—is overtaken by divinely kindled intensity. Reading change: the fire is a calibrated intensification of the social heat the offender helped produce.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c0edb9595bc99a3c43bb","text":"3. **Fire as illumination into the heart.** Evidence: `ن و ر B001` (light/illumination), `و ق د B006` (gleaming like kindled fire), activated by `ط ل ع B003` and `ف ء د B002` in 104:7. Structural cue: 104:6 names the fire; 104:7 describes what it does. Assigned role: light makes the inward target visible. Abductive move: a fire that reaches hearts must also reveal, not only consume, the interior it reaches. Reading change: the ayah reads diagnostically: divine fire illuminates the hidden account at the center of the person.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dbbc37862247fba9a999","text":"104:8-9 changed the fire from a passing flame into a maintained furnace: مؤصدة and عمد ممددة give it duration, pressure, and structure. The basmalah also stayed active: Allah's name here did not cancel mercy but showed that the same named authority governs both mercy and exact exposure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fbf4348c675628df87b9","text":"3. **Social enmity returned as a fully kindled process.** Fixed `نَارُ` activates `ن و ر B007`, enmity flaring between people, and fixed `ٱلْمُوقَدَةُ` activates `و ق د B005`, readiness to flare in anger or conflict. `104:1` supplies the initial interpersonal spark through `ه م ز B004` and `ل م ز B001`; `104:7` moves the resulting fire to the hearts. The social-to-thermal escalation is my inferred arrow. The line can thus read as hostile relation brought to its mature energetic form, now placed under divine attribution and turned back upon its inward source.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
